// QT Clustering Benchmark - Simplified Sequential Version
// 
// QT (Quality Threshold) clustering is an algorithm that builds clusters
// by starting with a seed point and iteratively adding the closest point
// that maintains the cluster's diameter below a threshold.

#include <algorithm>
#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <limits>
#include <vector>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

static const double MAX_WIDTH = 20.0;
static const double MAX_HEIGHT = 20.0;

// Structure to represent a point in 2D space
struct Point {
    double x, y;
};

// Structure to represent a cluster
struct Cluster {
    std::vector<int> members;
    int seed_point;
};

// Generate synthetic 2D point data in clusters
void generateSyntheticData(std::vector<Point>& points, const int N, unsigned int seed = 42) {
    auto frand = [&seed]() mutable { return rand_r(&seed) / static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX); };
    
    const double min_dim = std::min(MAX_WIDTH, MAX_HEIGHT);
    int count = 0;
    
    while (count < N) {
        // Create group_cnt points within a circle of radius R
        // around center point (cntr_x, cntr_y)
        const double cntr_x = frand() * MAX_WIDTH;
        const double cntr_y = frand() * MAX_HEIGHT;
        const double R = frand() * min_dim / 2.0;
        int group_cnt = static_cast<int>(frand() * (N / 30.0));
        
        // Make sure we don't make more points than we need
        if (group_cnt > (N - count)) {
            group_cnt = N - count;
        }
        
        while (group_cnt > 0) {
            const double sign = (frand() < 0.5) ? -1.0 : 1.0;
            const double r = frand() * R;
            const double dx = (2.0 * frand() - 1.0) * r;
            const double dy = std::sqrt(r * r - dx * dx) * sign;
            const double x = cntr_x + dx;
            const double y = cntr_y + dy;
            
            if (x < 0 || x > MAX_WIDTH || y < 0 || y > MAX_HEIGHT) {
                continue;
            }
            
            points[count] = {x, y};
            count++;
            group_cnt--;
        }
    }
}

// Calculate Euclidean distance between two points
inline double distance(const Point& p1, const Point& p2) {
    double dx = p1.x - p2.x;
    double dy = p1.y - p2.y;
    return std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);
}

// Find the closest unclustered point to the current cluster that maintains diameter < threshold
// Returns -1 if no such point exists
int findClosestPoint(const std::vector<int>& cluster_members,
                     const std::vector<bool>& clustered,
                     const std::vector<bool>& in_cluster,
                     const std::vector<Point>& points,
                     const double threshold,
                     const int point_count) {
    int closest_point = -1;
    double min_diameter = std::numeric_limits<double>::max();
    
    // Try each unclustered point as a candidate
    for (int candidate = 0; candidate < point_count; ++candidate) {
        // Skip if already clustered or already in this cluster
        if (clustered[candidate] || in_cluster[candidate]) continue;
        
        // Calculate the maximum distance from candidate to all cluster members
        double max_dist = 0.0;
        for (size_t i = 0; i < cluster_members.size(); ++i) {
            const int member = cluster_members[i];
            const double dist = distance(points[candidate], points[member]);
            max_dist = std::max(max_dist, dist);
        }
        
        // If adding this point keeps diameter below threshold and is better than current best
        if (max_dist < threshold && max_dist < min_diameter) {
            min_diameter = max_dist;
            closest_point = candidate;
        }
    }
    
    return closest_point;
}

// Generate a candidate cluster starting from a seed point
// Returns the cardinality (size) of the cluster
int generateCandidateCluster(const int seed_point,
                              const std::vector<bool>& clustered,
                              const std::vector<Point>& points,
                              const double threshold,
                              const int point_count,
                              std::vector<int>* cluster_members = nullptr) {
    std::vector<bool> in_cluster(point_count, false);
    std::vector<int> members;
    
    // Add seed point
    in_cluster[seed_point] = true;
    members.push_back(seed_point);
    
    // Iteratively add closest points
    while (static_cast<int>(members.size()) < point_count) {
        // Find closest point to current cluster that maintains diameter < threshold
        const int closest = findClosestPoint(members, clustered, in_cluster, points, 
                                             threshold, point_count);
        
        if (closest < 0) break; // No more points can be added
        
        in_cluster[closest] = true;
        members.push_back(closest);
    }
    
    // Copy members if requested
    if (cluster_members) {
        *cluster_members = members;
    }
    
    return static_cast<int>(members.size());
}

// Main QT clustering algorithm
std::vector<Cluster> qtClustering(const std::vector<Point>& points,
                                  const double threshold) {
    const int N = static_cast<int>(points.size());
    std::vector<bool> clustered(N, false);
    std::vector<int> unclustered_indices;
    std::vector<Cluster> clusters;
    
    // Initialize unclustered indices
    for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
        unclustered_indices.push_back(i);
    }
    
    // Main clustering loop
    while (!unclustered_indices.empty()) {
        int max_cardinality = -1;
        int best_seed = -1;
        std::vector<int> best_cluster_members;
        
        // Try each unclustered point as a seed
        for (size_t i = 0; i < unclustered_indices.size(); ++i) {
            const int seed = unclustered_indices[i];
            if (clustered[seed]) continue;
            
            std::vector<int> candidate_members;
            const int cardinality = generateCandidateCluster(seed, clustered, points, 
                                                       threshold, N, 
                                                       &candidate_members);
            
            if (cardinality > max_cardinality) {
                max_cardinality = cardinality;
                best_seed = seed;
                best_cluster_members = candidate_members;
            }
        }
        
        // If we found a cluster, add it
        if (best_seed >= 0 && max_cardinality > 0) {
            Cluster cluster;
            cluster.seed_point = best_seed;
            cluster.members = best_cluster_members;
            clusters.push_back(cluster);
            
            // Mark all members as clustered
            for (size_t i = 0; i < best_cluster_members.size(); ++i) {
                clustered[best_cluster_members[i]] = true;
            }
            
            // Remove clustered points from unclustered list
            unclustered_indices.erase(
                std::remove_if(unclustered_indices.begin(), unclustered_indices.end(),
                              [&clustered](int idx) { return clustered[idx]; }),
                unclustered_indices.end()
            );
        } else {
            // No more clusters can be formed
            break;
        }
    }
    
    return clusters;
}

// Validation: check that clusters satisfy the QT clustering properties
bool validateClusters(const std::vector<Cluster>& clusters,
                     const std::vector<Point>& points,
                     const double threshold) {
    bool valid = true;
    
    printf("Validating clusters:\n");
    
    // Check each cluster
    for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {
        const auto& cluster = clusters[c];
        double max_diameter = 0.0;
        
        // Check diameter (max distance between any two points)
        for (size_t i = 0; i < cluster.members.size(); ++i) {
            for (size_t j = i + 1; j < cluster.members.size(); ++j) {
                const double dist = distance(points[cluster.members[i]], 
                                           points[cluster.members[j]]);
                max_diameter = std::max(max_diameter, dist);
            }
        }
        
        if (c < 10) { // Print first 10 clusters
            printf("  Cluster %zu: size=%zu, seed=%d, diameter=%.4f\n", 
                   c, cluster.members.size(), cluster.seed_point, max_diameter);
        }
        
        // Validate diameter is within threshold
        if (max_diameter > threshold * 1.001) { // Allow small numerical error
            printf("ERROR: Cluster %zu has diameter %.4f > threshold %.4f\n", 
                   c, max_diameter, threshold);
            valid = false;
        }
    }
    
    // Check for duplicate memberships
    std::vector<int> membership(points.size(), -1);
    for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {
        for (size_t i = 0; i < clusters[c].members.size(); ++i) {
            const int member = clusters[c].members[i];
            if (membership[member] >= 0) {
                printf("ERROR: Point %d appears in multiple clusters (%d and %zu)\n",
                       member, membership[member], c);
                valid = false;
            }
            membership[member] = static_cast<int>(c);
        }
    }
    
    // Count clustered points
    int clustered_count = 0;

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -n <num>     Number of points (default: 1000)\n");
    printf("  -t <float>   Distance threshold for clustering (default: 2.0)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    int num_points = 1000;
    double threshold = 2.0;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;
    
    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            num_points = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-t") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            threshold = atof(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    if (num_points <= 0 || threshold <= 0.0) {
        printf("Error: Invalid parameters (num_points=%d, threshold=%.2f)\n", 
               num_points, threshold);
        return 1;
    }
    
    printf("QT Clustering Benchmark\n");
    printf("Number of points: %d\n", num_points);
    printf("Distance threshold: %.2f\n", threshold);
    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
    
    // Generate synthetic data
    std::vector<Point> points(num_points);
    generateSyntheticData(points, num_points);
    
    // Perform QT clustering
    auto cluster_start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    
    const std::vector<Cluster> clusters = qtClustering(points, threshold);
    
    auto cluster_end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    auto cluster_time = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(
        cluster_end - cluster_start);
    
    printf("Clustering time: %ld ms\n", cluster_time.count());
    printf("Clusters found: %zu\n", clusters.size());
    
    // Calculate statistics and performance metrics
    int total_clustered = 0;
    int max_cluster_size = 0;
    
    for (size_t i = 0; i < clusters.size(); ++i) {
        const int size = static_cast<int>(clusters[i].members.size());
        total_clustered += size;
        max_cluster_size = std::max(max_cluster_size, size);
    }
    
    const double avg_cluster_size = clusters.empty() ? 0.0 : 
        static_cast<double>(total_clustered) / clusters.size();
    
    printf("Points clustered: %d / %d (%.1f%%)\n", 
           total_clustered, num_points, 
           100.0 * total_clustered / num_points);
    printf("Average cluster size: %.2f\n", avg_cluster_size);
    printf("Maximum cluster size: %d\n", max_cluster_size);
    
    // Performance metrics
    const double time_sec = cluster_time.count() / 1000.0;
    const double clusters_per_sec = clusters.size() / time_sec;
    const double points_per_sec = num_points / time_sec;
    printf("Performance: %.1f clusters/s, %.1f points/s\n", 
           clusters_per_sec, points_per_sec);
    
    // Print results for external validation
    if (printResults) {
        // Serialize cluster membership for hashing
        std::vector<double> membershipData;
        membershipData.reserve(num_points);
        std::vector<int> membership(num_points, -1);
        for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {
            for (size_t i = 0; i < clusters[c].members.size(); ++i) {
                membership[clusters[c].members[i]] = static_cast<int>(c);
            }
        }
        for (int m : membership) {
            membershipData.push_back(static_cast<double>(m));
        }
        print_results(membershipData, "ClusterMembership");
    }
    
    // Validation
    if (validate) {
        const bool valid = validateClusters(clusters, points, threshold);
        
        if (valid) {
            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    return 0;
}
#pragma once

#include <algorithm>
#include <cinttypes>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <vector>

#include "hash.hpp"

// Kahan summation for numerically stable sum
template <typename T>
T kahan_sum(const std::vector<T>& data) {
    T sum = 0.0;
    T c = 0.0;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        T y = val - c;
        T t = sum + y;
        c = (t - sum) - y;
        sum = t;
    }
    return sum;
}

// Print results for floating-point arrays
template <typename T>
void print_results(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute statistics
    T sum = kahan_sum(data);
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Sum: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(sum));
    printf("Min: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %.17e\n", indices[i], static_cast<double>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}

// Print results for integer arrays (uses exact hash comparison)
template <typename T>
void print_results_int(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Compute min/max
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Min: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %lld\n", indices[i], static_cast<long long>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}
#pragma once

#include <cstdint>

// FNV-1a hash, 64 bit length
class hash {
  public:
	using digest = uint64_t;

	template <typename T>
	void add(const T& value) {
		const unsigned char* bytes = reinterpret_cast<const unsigned char*>(&value);
		for(size_t i = 0; i < sizeof(T); ++i) {
			d = (d ^ bytes[i]) * 0x100000001b3ull;
		}
	}

	digest get() const { return d; }

  private:
	digest d = 0xcbf29ce484222325ull;
};

cmake_minimum_required(VERSION 3.10)

project(qtclustering)

set(CMAKE_CXX_STANDARD 20)
set(CMAKE_CXX_STANDARD_REQUIRED ON)

add_executable(qtclustering qtclustering.cpp)

target_compile_options(qtclustering PRIVATE -O3 -march=native -Wall -Wextra)

# Link math library
target_link_libraries(qtclustering m)
// QT Clustering Benchmark - Simplified Sequential Version
// 
// QT (Quality Threshold) clustering is an algorithm that builds clusters
// by starting with a seed point and iteratively adding the closest point
// that maintains the cluster's diameter below a threshold.

#include <algorithm>
#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <limits>
#include <vector>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

static const double MAX_WIDTH = 20.0;
static const double MAX_HEIGHT = 20.0;

// Structure to represent a point in 2D space
struct Point {
    double x, y;
};

// Structure to represent a cluster
struct Cluster {
    std::vector<int> members;
    int seed_point;
};

// Generate synthetic 2D point data in clusters
void generateSyntheticData(std::vector<Point>& points, const int N, unsigned int seed = 42) {
    auto frand = [&seed]() mutable { return rand_r(&seed) / static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX); };
    
    const double min_dim = std::min(MAX_WIDTH, MAX_HEIGHT);
    int count = 0;
    
    while (count < N) {
        // Create group_cnt points within a circle of radius R
        // around center point (cntr_x, cntr_y)
        const double cntr_x = frand() * MAX_WIDTH;
        const double cntr_y = frand() * MAX_HEIGHT;
        const double R = frand() * min_dim / 2.0;
        int group_cnt = static_cast<int>(frand() * (N / 30.0));
        
        // Make sure we don't make more points than we need
        if (group_cnt > (N - count)) {
            group_cnt = N - count;
        }
        
        while (group_cnt > 0) {
            const double sign = (frand() < 0.5) ? -1.0 : 1.0;
            const double r = frand() * R;
            const double dx = (2.0 * frand() - 1.0) * r;
            const double dy = std::sqrt(r * r - dx * dx) * sign;
            const double x = cntr_x + dx;
            const double y = cntr_y + dy;
            
            if (x < 0 || x > MAX_WIDTH || y < 0 || y > MAX_HEIGHT) {
                continue;
            }
            
            points[count] = {x, y};
            count++;
            group_cnt--;
        }
    }
}

// Calculate Euclidean distance between two points
inline double distance(const Point& p1, const Point& p2) {
    double dx = p1.x - p2.x;
    double dy = p1.y - p2.y;
    return std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);
}

// Find the closest unclustered point to the current cluster that maintains diameter < threshold
// Returns -1 if no such point exists
int findClosestPoint(const std::vector<int>& cluster_members,
                     const std::vector<bool>& clustered,
                     const std::vector<bool>& in_cluster,
                     const std::vector<Point>& points,
                     const double threshold,
                     const int point_count) {
    int closest_point = -1;
    double min_diameter = std::numeric_limits<double>::max();
    
    // Try each unclustered point as a candidate
    for (int candidate = 0; candidate < point_count; ++candidate) {
        // Skip if already clustered or already in this cluster
        if (clustered[candidate] || in_cluster[candidate]) continue;
        
        // Calculate the maximum distance from candidate to all cluster members
        double max_dist = 0.0;
        for (size_t i = 0; i < cluster_members.size(); ++i) {
            const int member = cluster_members[i];
            const double dist = distance(points[candidate], points[member]);
            max_dist = std::max(max_dist, dist);
        }
        
        // If adding this point keeps diameter below threshold and is better than current best
        if (max_dist < threshold && max_dist < min_diameter) {
            min_diameter = max_dist;
            closest_point = candidate;
        }
    }
    
    return closest_point;
}

// Generate a candidate cluster starting from a seed point
// Returns the cardinality (size) of the cluster
int generateCandidateCluster(const int seed_point,
                              const std::vector<bool>& clustered,
                              const std::vector<Point>& points,
                              const double threshold,
                              const int point_count,
                              std::vector<int>* cluster_members = nullptr) {
    std::vector<bool> in_cluster(point_count, false);
    std::vector<int> members;
    
    // Add seed point
    in_cluster[seed_point] = true;
    members.push_back(seed_point);
    
    // Iteratively add closest points
    while (static_cast<int>(members.size()) < point_count) {
        // Find closest point to current cluster that maintains diameter < threshold
        const int closest = findClosestPoint(members, clustered, in_cluster, points, 
                                             threshold, point_count);
        
        if (closest < 0) break; // No more points can be added
        
        in_cluster[closest] = true;
        members.push_back(closest);
    }
    
    // Copy members if requested
    if (cluster_members) {
        *cluster_members = members;
    }
    
    return static_cast<int>(members.size());
}

// Main QT clustering algorithm
std::vector<Cluster> qtClustering(const std::vector<Point>& points,
                                  const double threshold) {
    const int N = static_cast<int>(points.size());
    std::vector<bool> clustered(N, false);
    std::vector<int> unclustered_indices;
    std::vector<Cluster> clusters;
    
    // Initialize unclustered indices
    for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
        unclustered_indices.push_back(i);
    }
    
    // Main clustering loop
    while (!unclustered_indices.empty()) {
        int max_cardinality = -1;
        int best_seed = -1;
        std::vector<int> best_cluster_members;
        
        // Try each unclustered point as a seed
        for (size_t i = 0; i < unclustered_indices.size(); ++i) {
            const int seed = unclustered_indices[i];
            if (clustered[seed]) continue;
            
            std::vector<int> candidate_members;
            const int cardinality = generateCandidateCluster(seed, clustered, points, 
                                                       threshold, N, 
                                                       &candidate_members);
            
            if (cardinality > max_cardinality) {
                max_cardinality = cardinality;
                best_seed = seed;
                best_cluster_members = candidate_members;
            }
        }
        
        // If we found a cluster, add it
        if (best_seed >= 0 && max_cardinality > 0) {
            Cluster cluster;
            cluster.seed_point = best_seed;
            cluster.members = best_cluster_members;
            clusters.push_back(cluster);
            
            // Mark all members as clustered
            for (size_t i = 0; i < best_cluster_members.size(); ++i) {
                clustered[best_cluster_members[i]] = true;
            }
            
            // Remove clustered points from unclustered list
            unclustered_indices.erase(
                std::remove_if(unclustered_indices.begin(), unclustered_indices.end(),
                              [&clustered](int idx) { return clustered[idx]; }),
                unclustered_indices.end()
            );
        } else {
            // No more clusters can be formed
            break;
        }
    }
    
    return clusters;
}

// Validation: check that clusters satisfy the QT clustering properties
bool validateClusters(const std::vector<Cluster>& clusters,
                     const std::vector<Point>& points,
                     const double threshold) {
    bool valid = true;
    
    printf("Validating clusters:\n");
    
    // Check each cluster
    for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {
        const auto& cluster = clusters[c];
        double max_diameter = 0.0;
        
        // Check diameter (max distance between any two points)
        for (size_t i = 0; i < cluster.members.size(); ++i) {
            for (size_t j = i + 1; j < cluster.members.size(); ++j) {
                const double dist = distance(points[cluster.members[i]], 
                                           points[cluster.members[j]]);
                max_diameter = std::max(max_diameter, dist);
            }
        }
        
        if (c < 10) { // Print first 10 clusters
            printf("  Cluster %zu: size=%zu, seed=%d, diameter=%.4f\n", 
                   c, cluster.members.size(), cluster.seed_point, max_diameter);
        }
        
        // Validate diameter is within threshold
        if (max_diameter > threshold * 1.001) { // Allow small numerical error
            printf("ERROR: Cluster %zu has diameter %.4f > threshold %.4f\n", 
                   c, max_diameter, threshold);
            valid = false;
        }
    }
    
    // Check for duplicate memberships
    std::vector<int> membership(points.size(), -1);
    for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {
        for (size_t i = 0; i < clusters[c].members.size(); ++i) {
            const int member = clusters[c].members[i];
            if (membership[member] >= 0) {
                printf("ERROR: Point %d appears in multiple clusters (%d and %zu)\n",
                       member, membership[member], c);
                valid = false;
            }
            membership[member] = static_cast<int>(c);
        }
    }
    
    // Count clustered points
    int clustered_count = 0;

// Main QT clustering algorithm
std::vector<Cluster> qtClustering(const std::vector<Point>& points,
                                  const double threshold) {
    const int N = static_cast<int>(points.size());
    std::vector<bool> clustered(N, false);
    std::vector<int> unclustered_indices;
    std::vector<Cluster> clusters;
    
    // Initialize unclustered indices
    for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
        unclustered_indices.push_back(i);
    }
    
    // Main clustering loop
    while (!unclustered_indices.empty()) {
        int max_cardinality = -1;
        int best_seed = -1;
        std::vector<int> best_cluster_members;
        
        // Try each unclustered point as a seed
        for (size_t i = 0; i < unclustered_indices.size(); ++i) {
            const int seed = unclustered_indices[i];
            if (clustered[seed]) continue;
            
            std::vector<int> candidate_members;
            const int cardinality = generateCandidateCluster(seed, clustered, points, 
                                                       threshold, N, 
                                                       &candidate_members);
            
            if (cardinality > max_cardinality) {
                max_cardinality = cardinality;
                best_seed = seed;
                best_cluster_members = candidate_members;
            }
        }
        
        // If we found a cluster, add it
        if (best_seed >= 0 && max_cardinality > 0) {
            Cluster cluster;
            cluster.seed_point = best_seed;
            cluster.members = best_cluster_members;
            clusters.push_back(cluster);
            
            // Mark all members as clustered
            for (size_t i = 0; i < best_cluster_members.size(); ++i) {
                clustered[best_cluster_members[i]] = true;
            }
            
            // Remove clustered points from unclustered list
            unclustered_indices.erase(
                std::remove_if(unclustered_indices.begin(), unclustered_indices.end(),
                              [&clustered](int idx) { return clustered[idx]; }),
                unclustered_indices.end()
            );
        } else {
            // No more clusters can be formed
            break;
        }
    }
    
    return clusters;
}

// Validation: check that clusters satisfy the QT clustering properties
bool validateClusters(const std::vector<Cluster>& clusters,
                     const std::vector<Point>& points,
                     const double threshold) {
    bool valid = true;
    
    printf("Validating clusters:\n");
    
    // Check each cluster
    for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {
        const auto& cluster = clusters[c];
        double max_diameter = 0.0;
        
        // Check diameter (max distance between any two points)
        for (size_t i = 0; i < cluster.members.size(); ++i) {
            for (size_t j = i + 1; j < cluster.members.size(); ++j) {
                const double dist = distance(points[cluster.members[i]], 
                                           points[cluster.members[j]]);
                max_diameter = std::max(max_diameter, dist);
            }
        }
        
        if (c < 10) { // Print first 10 clusters
            printf("  Cluster %zu: size=%zu, seed=%d, diameter=%.4f\n", 
                   c, cluster.members.size(), cluster.seed_point, max_diameter);
        }
        
        // Validate diameter is within threshold
        if (max_diameter > threshold * 1.001) { // Allow small numerical error
            printf("ERROR: Cluster %zu has diameter %.4f > threshold %.4f\n", 
                   c, max_diameter, threshold);
            valid = false;
        }
    }
    
    // Check for duplicate memberships
    std::vector<int> membership(points.size(), -1);
    for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {
        for (size_t i = 0; i < clusters[c].members.size(); ++i) {
            const int member = clusters[c].members[i];
            if (membership[member] >= 0) {
                printf("ERROR: Point %d appears in multiple clusters (%d and %zu)\n",
                       member, membership[member], c);
                valid = false;
            }
            membership[member] = static_cast<int>(c);
        }
    }
    
    // Count clustered points
    int clustered_count = 0;
    for (size_t i = 0; i < membership.size(); ++i) {
        if (membership[i] >= 0) clustered_count++;
    }
    
    printf("Total points: %zu, Clustered: %d, Unclustered: %zu\n",
           points.size(), clustered_count, points.size() - clustered_count);
    
    return valid;
}

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -n <num>     Number of points (default: 1000)\n");
    printf("  -t <float>   Distance threshold for clustering (default: 2.0)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    int num_points = 1000;
    double threshold = 2.0;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;
    
    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            num_points = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-t") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            threshold = atof(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    if (num_points <= 0 || threshold <= 0.0) {
        printf("Error: Invalid parameters (num_points=%d, threshold=%.2f)\n", 
               num_points, threshold);
        return 1;
    }
    
    printf("QT Clustering Benchmark\n");
    printf("Number of points: %d\n", num_points);
    printf("Distance threshold: %.2f\n", threshold);
    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
    
    // Generate synthetic data
    std::vector<Point> points(num_points);
    generateSyntheticData(points, num_points);
    
    // Perform QT clustering
    auto cluster_start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    
    const std::vector<Cluster> clusters = qtClustering(points, threshold);
    
    auto cluster_end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    auto cluster_time = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(
        cluster_end - cluster_start);
    
    printf("Clustering time: %ld ms\n", cluster_time.count());
    printf("Clusters found: %zu\n", clusters.size());
    
    // Calculate statistics and performance metrics
    int total_clustered = 0;
    int max_cluster_size = 0;
    
    for (size_t i = 0; i < clusters.size(); ++i) {
        const int size = static_cast<int>(clusters[i].members.size());
        total_clustered += size;
        max_cluster_size = std::max(max_cluster_size, size);
    }
    
    const double avg_cluster_size = clusters.empty() ? 0.0 : 
        static_cast<double>(total_clustered) / clusters.size();
    
    printf("Points clustered: %d / %d (%.1f%%)\n", 
           total_clustered, num_points, 
           100.0 * total_clustered / num_points);
    printf("Average cluster size: %.2f\n", avg_cluster_size);
    printf("Maximum cluster size: %d\n", max_cluster_size);
    
    // Performance metrics
    const double time_sec = cluster_time.count() / 1000.0;
    const double clusters_per_sec = clusters.size() / time_sec;
    const double points_per_sec = num_points / time_sec;
    printf("Performance: %.1f clusters/s, %.1f points/s\n", 
           clusters_per_sec, points_per_sec);
    
    // Print results for external validation
    if (printResults) {
        // Serialize cluster membership for hashing
        std::vector<double> membershipData;
        membershipData.reserve(num_points);
        std::vector<int> membership(num_points, -1);
        for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {
            for (size_t i = 0; i < clusters[c].members.size(); ++i) {
                membership[clusters[c].members[i]] = static_cast<int>(c);
            }
        }
        for (int m : membership) {
            membershipData.push_back(static_cast<double>(m));
        }
        print_results(membershipData, "ClusterMembership");
    }
    
    // Validation
    if (validate) {
        const bool valid = validateClusters(clusters, points, threshold);
        
        if (valid) {
            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    return 0;
}

I’ll retain candidate results between rounds and rebuild only those whose members were removed. Removing other points cannot change a candidate’s choices, so this is safe and should save substantial work. GPUs will also use memory-bounded batches when the neighbor graph is too large to keep resident.

/bin/bash -lc "python3 - <<'PY'
from pathlib import Path
p = Path('qtclustering/qtclustering.cpp')
s = p.read_text()
s = s.replace('// QT Clustering Benchmark - Simplified Sequential Version', '// QT Clustering Benchmark - MPI / OpenMP / CUDA')
s = s.replace('#include <limits>\\n#include <vector>', '#include <limits>\\n#include <memory>\\n#include <stdexcept>\\n#include <string>\\n#include <vector>\\n\\n#include <cuda_runtime.h>\\n#include <mpi.h>\\n#include <omp.h>')
s = s.replace('        // Make sure we don\\'t make more points than we need', '        // For N <= 30 the original expression always produces zero.\\n        if (N <= 30) group_cnt = 1;\\n\\n        // Make sure we don\\'t make more points than we need')
start = s.index('// Calculate Euclidean distance')
end = s.index('// Validation: check that clusters')
s = s[:start] + r'''// Keep the same double-precision distances, strict threshold, and lowest-index
// tie breaking as the sequential algorithm. In particular, comparing squared
// distances instead would change ties caused by square-root rounding.
__host__ __device__ inline double distance(const Point& p1, const Point& p2) {
    const double dx = p1.x - p2.x;
    const double dy = p1.y - p2.y;
#ifdef __CUDA_ARCH__
    return __dsqrt_rn(dx * dx + dy * dy);
#else
    return std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);
#endif
}

static void checkCuda(cudaError_t status) {
    if (status "'!= cudaSuccess) throw std::runtime_error(cudaGetErrorString(status));
}

static void checkMpi(int status) {
    if (status != MPI_SUCCESS) {
        char message[MPI_MAX_ERROR_STRING];
        int length = 0;
        MPI_Error_string(status, message, &length);
        fprintf(stderr, "MPI: %.*s'"\\n\", length, message);
        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, status);
    }
}

// MPI calls stay on the initial thread; OpenMP workers manage independent CUDA
// devices. A rank can use several GPUs, and local ranks divide visible devices.
struct ParallelRuntime {
    int rank = 0, ranks = 1, local_rank = 0, local_ranks = 1;
    std::vector<int> devices;
    int device_sharers = 1;

    ParallelRuntime(int& argc, char**& argv) {
        int provided = MPI_THREAD_SINGLE;
        checkMpi(MPI_Init_thread(&argc, &argv, MPI_THREAD_FUNNELED, &provided));
        checkMpi(MPI_Comm_set_errhandler(MPI_COMM_WORLD, MPI_ERRORS_RETURN));
        checkMpi(MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank));
        checkMpi(MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &ranks));
        if (provided < MPI_THREAD_FUNNELED) {
            if (rank == 0) fprintf(stderr, \"MPI_THREAD_FUNNELED is required.\\n\");
            MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);
        }
    }

    void initializeDevices() {
        MPI_Comm local_comm;
        checkMpi(MPI_Comm_split_type(MPI_COMM_WORLD, MPI_COMM_TYPE_SHARED, rank,
                                    MPI_INFO_NULL, &local_comm));
        checkMpi(MPI_Comm_rank(local_comm, &local_rank));
        checkMpi(MPI_Comm_size(local_comm, &local_ranks));
        checkMpi(MPI_Comm_free(&local_comm));
        int count = 0;
        checkCuda(cudaGetDeviceCount(&count));
        if (count == 0) throw std::runtime_error(\"CUDA requires at least one visible GPU per rank\");
        if (local_ranks <= count) {
            for (int device = local_rank; device < count; device += local_ranks)
                devices.push_back(device);
        } else {
            devices.push_back(local_rank % count);
            device_sharers = (local_ranks - 1 - devices[0]) / count + 1;
        }
        std::vector<std::string> errors(devices.size());
        // Establish CUDA contexts before the benchmark timer starts.
        #pragma omp parallel for num_threads(static_cast<int>(devices.size())) schedule(static)
        for (int i = 0; i < static_cast<int>(devices.size()); ++i) {
            try {
                checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(devices[i]));
                checkCuda(cudaFree(nullptr));
            } catch (const std::exception& e) { errors[i] = e.what(); }
        }
        for (const auto& error : errors)
            if ("'!error.empty()) throw std::runtime_error(error);
    }

    ~ParallelRuntime() { MPI_Finalize(); }
};

constexpr int BLOCK_SIZE = 128;
constexpr int SHARED_CANDIDATES = 1024;
using EdgeIndex = unsigned long long;

// Warp/block reductions compare both distance and point index, independently
// of graph storage order and GPU scheduling.
__device__ inline void reduceClosest(double& value, int& point) {
    const int lane = threadIdx.x & 31;
    const int warp = threadIdx.x >> 5;
    for (int shift = 16; shift; shift >>= 1) {
        const double other_value = __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffff, value, shift);
        const int other_point = __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffff, point, shift);
        if (other_value < value || (other_value == value && other_point < point)) {
            value = other_value;
            point = other_point;
        }
    }
    __shared__ double warp_values[BLOCK_SIZE / 32];
    __shared__ int warp_points[BLOCK_SIZE / 32];
    if (lane == 0) { warp_values[warp] = value; warp_points[warp] = point; }
    __syncthreads();
    if (warp == 0) {
        value = lane < BLOCK_SIZE / 32 ? warp_values[lane] : DBL_MAX;
        point = lane < BLOCK_SIZE / 32 ? warp_points[lane] : INT_MAX;
        for (int shift = 16; shift; shift >>= 1) {
            const double other_value = __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffff, value, shift);
            const int other_point = __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffff, point, shift);
            if (other_value < value || (other_value == value && other_point < point)) {
                value = other_value;
                point = other_point;
            }
        }
        if (lane == 0) { warp_values[0] = value; warp_points[0] = point; }
    }
    __syncthreads();
    value = warp_values[0];
    point = warp_points[0];
}

__global__ void countNeighbors(const Point* points, int n, double threshold,
                               long long first_seed, long long stride,
                               int* counts, int rows) {
    const int row = blockIdx.x;
    if (row >= rows) return;
    const int seed = static_cast<int>(first_seed + row * stride);
    const Point center = points[seed];
    int count = 0;
    for (long long p = threadIdx.x; p < n; p += BLOCK_SIZE)
        count += p != seed && distance(center, points[p]) < threshold;
    for (int shift = 16; shift; shift >>= 1)
        count += __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffff, count, shift);
    __shared__ int warp_counts[BLOCK_SIZE / 32];
    if ((threadIdx.x & 31) == 0) warp_counts[threadIdx.x >> 5] = count;
    __syncthreads();
    if (threadIdx.x == 0) {
        int total = 0;
        for (int w = 0; w < BLOCK_SIZE / 32; ++w) total += warp_counts[w];
        counts[row] = total;
    }
}

// Construct just the threshold neighborhood of each owned seed. Warp ballots
// compact each tile without a global atomic or a dense N x N distance matrix.
__global__ void fillNeighbors(const Point* points, int n, double threshold,
                              long long first_seed, long long stride,
                              const EdgeIndex* offsets, int first_row, int rows,
                              EdgeIndex edge_base, int* neighbors) {
    const int row = first_row + blockIdx.x;
    if (row >= first_row + rows) return;
    const int seed = static_cast<int>(first_seed + row * stride);
    const Point center = points[seed];
    const EdgeIndex start = offsets[row] - edge_base;
    const int lane = threadIdx.x & 31, warp = threadIdx.x >> 5;
    __shared__ int warp_offsets[BLOCK_SIZE / 32];
    __shared__ int written;
    if (threadIdx.x == 0) written = 0;
    __syncthreads();
    for (long long tile = 0; tile < n; tile += BLOCK_SIZE) {
        const long long p = tile + threadIdx.x;
        const bool eligible = p < n && p != seed && distance(center, points[p]) < threshold;
        const unsigned mask = __ballot_sync(0xffffffff, eligible);
        if (lane == 0) warp_offsets[warp] = __popc(mask);
        __syncthreads();
        if (threadIdx.x == 0) {
            int next = written;
            for (int w = 0; w < BLOCK_SIZE / 32; ++w) {
                const int count = warp_offsets[w];
                warp_offsets[w] = next;
                next += count;
            }
            written = next;
        }
        __syncthreads();
        if (eligible) {
            const unsigned lower_lanes = (1u << lane) - 1u;
            neighbors[start + warp_offsets[warp] + __popc(mask & lower_lanes)] = static_cast<int>(p);
        }
        __syncthreads();
    }
}

// One cooperative block grows a seed'"'s candidate. Scores are updated against
// only the newest member, changing repeated O(k"'^2) distance work to O(k).
// A negative score records a selected member'"'s position, preserving its order
// without another membership array. DBL_MAX denotes a permanently pruned point.
// Small neighborhoods stay in shared memory throughout candidate growth.
template<bool use_shared>
__global__ void growCandidates(const Point* points, double threshold,
                               long long first_seed, long long stride,
                               const unsigned char* clustered,
                               const EdgeIndex* offsets, int first_row, int rows,
                               EdgeIndex edge_base, const int* neighbors,
                               double* scores, int* sizes, int shared_capacity,
                               bool reuse) {
    const int row = first_row + blockIdx.x;
    if (row >= first_row + rows) return;
    const EdgeIndex start = offsets[row] - edge_base;
    const int count = static_cast<int>(offsets[row + 1] - offsets[row]);
    if (use_shared ? count > shared_capacity : count <= shared_capacity) return;
    const int seed = static_cast<int>(first_seed + row * stride);
    if (clustered[seed]) {
        if (threadIdx.x == 0) sizes[row] = 0;
        return;
    }

    // Removing points outside a cached candidate leaves every greedy choice
    // unchanged. Rebuild only when one of that candidate's members was removed.
    if (reuse && sizes[row] >= 0) {
        bool removed = false;
        for (int j = threadIdx.x; j < count; j += BLOCK_SIZE)
            if (scores[start + j] < 0.0 && clustered[neighbors[start + j]]) removed = true;
        if ("'!__syncthreads_or(removed)) return;
    }

    extern __shared__ double shared_data[];
    double* values = use_shared ? shared_data : scores + start;
    double* x = shared_data + shared_capacity;
    double* y = x + shared_capacity;
    int* ids = reinterpret_cast<int*>(y + shared_capacity);
    for (int j = threadIdx.x; j < count; j += BLOCK_SIZE) {
        const int p = neighbors[start + j];
        values[j] = clustered[p] ? DBL_MAX : 0.0;
        if (use_shared) { ids[j] = p; x[j] = points[p].x; y[j] = points[p].y; }
    }
    __syncthreads();

    int cardinality = 1, newest = seed;
    for (;;) {
        double closest_distance = DBL_MAX;
        int closest_point = INT_MAX;
        const Point member = points[newest];
        for (int j = threadIdx.x; j < count; j += BLOCK_SIZE) {
            double score = values[j];
            if (score < 0.0 || score == DBL_MAX) continue;
            const int p = use_shared ? ids[j] : neighbors[start + j];
            if (p == newest) {
                values[j] = -static_cast<double>(cardinality - 1);
                continue;
            }
            Point candidate;
            if (use_shared) candidate = {x[j], y[j]};
            else candidate = points[p];
            const double d = distance(candidate, member);
            score = fmax(score, d);
            if (!(score < threshold)) { values[j] = DBL_MAX; continue; }
            values[j] = score;
            if (score < closest_distance || (score == closest_distance && p < closest_point)) {
                closest_distance = score;
                closest_point = p;
            }
        }
        reduceClosest(closest_distance, closest_point);
        if (closest_point == INT_MAX) break;
        newest = closest_point;
        ++cardinality;
    }
    if (use_shared)
        for (int j = threadIdx.x; j < count; j += BLOCK_SIZE) scores[start + j] = values[j];
    if (threadIdx.x == 0) sizes[row] = cardinality;
}

__global__ void extractMembers(long long first_seed, long long stride,
                               const EdgeIndex* offsets, int row, EdgeIndex edge_base,
                               const int* neighbors, const double* scores, int* members) {
    if (threadIdx.x == 0) members[0] = static_cast<int>(first_seed + row * stride);
    const EdgeIndex start = offsets[row] - edge_base;
    const EdgeIndex end = offsets[row + 1] - edge_base;
    for (EdgeIndex j = start + threadIdx.x; j < end; j += BLOCK_SIZE)
        if (scores[j] < 0.0) members[static_cast<int>(-scores[j])] = neighbors[j];
}

__global__ void markClustered(unsigned char* clustered, const int* members, int count) {
    const int i = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
    if (i < count) clustered[members[i]] = 1;
}

struct SeedChoice {
    int size = 0;
    int seed = INT_MAX;
};

// Every device owns cyclically distributed seeds, so input generation order
// and the changing number of unclustered points do not concentrate work on one
// rank. Only points and the accepted cluster are replicated across ranks.
class DeviceCandidates {
    struct Batch { int first, rows; };
    int device_, n_, rows_, shared_capacity_ = 0;
    long long first_seed_, stride_;
    double threshold_;
    cudaStream_t stream_ = nullptr;
    Point* points_ = nullptr;
    unsigned char* clustered_ = nullptr;
    EdgeIndex* offsets_ = nullptr;
    int* neighbors_ = nullptr;
    double* scores_ = nullptr;
    int* sizes_ = nullptr;
    int* members_ = nullptr;
    int* host_sizes_ = nullptr;
    std::vector<EdgeIndex> host_offsets_;
    std::vector<Batch> batches_;
    bool resident_ = true;

    void fill(const Batch& batch) {
        if (host_offsets_[batch.first + batch.rows] == host_offsets_[batch.first]) return;
        fillNeighbors<<<batch.rows, BLOCK_SIZE, 0, stream_>>>(
            points_, n_, threshold_, first_seed_, stride_, offsets_, batch.first,
            batch.rows, host_offsets_[batch.first], neighbors_);
        checkCuda(cudaGetLastError());
    }

    void grow(const Batch& batch, bool reuse) {
        const EdgeIndex base = host_offsets_[batch.first];
        growCandidates<true><<<batch.rows, BLOCK_SIZE, shared_capacity_ * (3 * sizeof(double) + sizeof(int)), stream_>>>(
            points_, threshold_, first_seed_, stride_, clustered_, offsets_, batch.first,
            batch.rows, base, neighbors_, scores_, sizes_, shared_capacity_, reuse);
        checkCuda(cudaGetLastError());
        if (shared_capacity_ == SHARED_CANDIDATES) {
            growCandidates<false><<<batch.rows, BLOCK_SIZE, 0, stream_>>>(
                points_, threshold_, first_seed_, stride_, clustered_, offsets_, batch.first,
                batch.rows, base, neighbors_, scores_, sizes_, shared_capacity_, reuse);
            checkCuda(cudaGetLastError());
        }
    }

public:
    DeviceCandidates(int device, int n, int rows, long long first_seed,
                     long long stride, double threshold)
        : device_(device), n_(n), rows_(rows), first_seed_(first_seed),
          stride_(stride), threshold_(threshold) {}

    void initialize(const std::vector<Point>& points, int sharers) {
        checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(device_));
        checkCuda(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&stream_, cudaStreamNonBlocking));
        checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&points_, static_cast<size_t>(n_) * sizeof(Point)));
        checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&clustered_, static_cast<size_t>(n_)));
        checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&members_, static_cast<size_t>(n_) * sizeof(int)));
        checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(points_, points.data(), static_cast<size_t>(n_) * sizeof(Point), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, stream_));
        checkCuda(cudaMemsetAsync(clustered_, 0, static_cast<size_t>(n_), stream_));
        if (rows_ == 0) { checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream_)); return; }
        checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&sizes_, static_cast<size_t>(rows_) * sizeof(int)));
        checkCuda(cudaMallocHost(&host_sizes_, static_cast<size_t>(rows_) * sizeof(int)));
        countNeighbors<<<rows_, BLOCK_SIZE, 0, stream_>>>(points_, n_, threshold_, first_seed_, stride_, sizes_, rows_);
        checkCuda(cudaGetLastError());
        checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(host_sizes_, sizes_, static_cast<size_t>(rows_) * sizeof(int), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, stream_));
        checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream_));
        host_offsets_.resize(static_cast<size_t>(rows_) + 1, 0);
        int max_degree = 0;
        for (int row = 0; row < rows_; ++row) {
            host_offsets_[row + 1] = host_offsets_[row] + host_sizes_[row];
            max_degree = std::max(max_degree, host_sizes_[row]);
        }
        shared_capacity_ = std::min(max_degree, SHARED_CANDIDATES);
        checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&offsets_, host_offsets_.size() * sizeof(EdgeIndex)));
        checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(offsets_, host_offsets_.data(), host_offsets_.size() * sizeof(EdgeIndex), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, stream_));
        size_t free_bytes = 0, total_bytes = 0;
        checkCuda(cudaMemGetInfo(&free_bytes, &total_bytes));
        const EdgeIndex capacity = (free_bytes / sharers / 4 * 3) / (sizeof(int) + sizeof(double));
        if (static_cast<EdgeIndex>(max_degree) > capacity)
            throw std::runtime_error("Insufficient GPU memory for a single seed'"'s neighborhood\");
        EdgeIndex largest_batch = 0;
        int first = 0;
        for (int row = 0; row < rows_; ++row) {
            if (host_offsets_[row + 1] - host_offsets_[first] > capacity) {
                batches_.push_back({first, row - first});
                largest_batch = std::max(largest_batch, host_offsets_[row] - host_offsets_[first]);
                first = row;
            }
        }
        batches_.push_back({first, rows_ - first});
        largest_batch = std::max(largest_batch, host_offsets_.back() - host_offsets_[first]);
        resident_ = batches_.size() == 1;
        // Allocate at least one element for zero-neighbor datasets.
        const size_t allocation = static_cast<size_t>(std::max<EdgeIndex>(1, largest_batch));
        checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&neighbors_, allocation * sizeof(int)));
        checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&scores_, allocation * sizeof(double)));
        checkCuda(cudaMemsetAsync(sizes_, 0xff, static_cast<size_t>(rows_) * sizeof(int), stream_));
        if (resident_) fill(batches_[0]);
        checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream_));
    }

    SeedChoice evaluate() {
        checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(device_));
        if (rows_ == 0) return {};
        for (const auto& batch : batches_) {
            if ("'!resident_) fill(batch);
            grow(batch, resident_);
        }
        checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(host_sizes_, sizes_, static_cast<size_t>(rows_) * sizeof(int), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, stream_));
        checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream_));
        SeedChoice best;
        for (int row = 0; row < rows_; ++row) {
            const int seed = static_cast<int>(first_seed_ + row * stride_);
            if (host_sizes_[row] > best.size || (host_sizes_[row] == best.size && seed < best.seed))
                best = {host_sizes_[row], seed};
        }
        return best;
    }

    void getMembers(int seed, std::vector<int>& members) {
        checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(device_));
        const int row = static_cast<int>((seed - first_seed_) / stride_);
        EdgeIndex base = 0;
        if (!resident_) {
            // A streamed batch has overwritten scratch storage. Rebuild only
            // the winning row, rather than retaining all candidates on the host.
            const Batch winner{row, 1};
            fill(winner);
            grow(winner, false);
            base = host_offsets_[row];
        }
        extractMembers<<<1, BLOCK_SIZE, 0, stream_>>>(first_seed_, stride_, offsets_, row, base, neighbors_, scores_, members_);
        checkCuda(cudaGetLastError());
        checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(members.data(), members_, members.size() * sizeof(int), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, stream_));
        checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream_));
    }

    void remove(const std::vector<int>& members) {
        checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(device_));
        if (rows_ == 0) return;
        checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(members_, members.data(), members.size() * sizeof(int), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, stream_));
        markClustered<<<(members.size() + BLOCK_SIZE - 1) / BLOCK_SIZE, BLOCK_SIZE, 0, stream_>>>(
            clustered_, members_, static_cast<int>(members.size()));
        checkCuda(cudaGetLastError());
        // The same stream orders this update before the next candidate round.
    }

    ~DeviceCandidates() {
        cudaSetDevice(device_);
        if (stream_) cudaStreamSynchronize(stream_);
        cudaFree(points_); cudaFree(clustered_); cudaFree(offsets_);
        cudaFree(neighbors_); cudaFree(scores_); cudaFree(sizes_); cudaFree(members_);
        if (host_sizes_) cudaFreeHost(host_sizes_);
        if (stream_) cudaStreamDestroy(stream_);
    }
};

// Workers never call MPI, including on CUDA failures. Exceptions are collected
// and propagated on the initial thread, where the caller can abort all ranks.
template<class Function>
static void forDevices(int count, Function function) {
    std::vector<std::string> errors(count);
    #pragma omp parallel for num_threads(count) schedule(static)
    for (int i = 0; i < count; ++i) {
        try { function(i); }
        catch (const std::exception& e) { errors[i] = e.what(); }
    }
    for (const auto& error : errors)
        if (!error.empty()) throw std::runtime_error(error);
}

std::vector<Cluster> qtClustering(const std::vector<Point>& points, double threshold,
                                  const ParallelRuntime& runtime) {
    const int n = static_cast<int>(points.size());
    const int device_count = static_cast<int>(runtime.devices.size());
    const int local_seeds = runtime.rank < n ? (n - 1 - runtime.rank) / runtime.ranks + 1 : 0;
    std::vector<std::unique_ptr<DeviceCandidates>> devices(device_count);
    for (int i = 0; i < device_count; ++i) {
        const int rows = i < local_seeds ? (local_seeds - 1 - i) / device_count + 1 : 0;
        devices[i] = std::make_unique<DeviceCandidates>(runtime.devices[i], n, rows,
            runtime.rank + static_cast<long long>(runtime.ranks) * i,
            static_cast<long long>(runtime.ranks) * device_count, threshold);
    }
    forDevices(device_count, [&](int i) { devices[i]->initialize(points, runtime.device_sharers); });
    std::vector<SeedChoice> choices(device_count);
    std::vector<Cluster> clusters;
    int remaining = n;
    while (remaining > 0) {
        forDevices(device_count, [&](int i) { choices[i] = devices[i]->evaluate(); });
        // MPI_MINLOC selects the largest size and then the smallest global seed,
        // exactly matching the sequential traversal through unclustered indices.
        int local_best[2] = {0, INT_MAX}, global_best[2];
        for (const auto& choice : choices)
            if (-choice.size < local_best[0] || (-choice.size == local_best[0] && choice.seed < local_best[1])) {
                local_best[0] = -choice.size;
                local_best[1] = choice.seed;
            }
        checkMpi(MPI_Allreduce(local_best, global_best, 1, MPI_2INT, MPI_MINLOC, MPI_COMM_WORLD));
        const int cardinality = -global_best[0], seed = global_best[1];
        if (cardinality <= 0 || cardinality > remaining || seed < 0 || seed >= n)
            throw std::runtime_error("Invalid global candidate selection");
        const int owner = seed % runtime.ranks;
        std::vector<int> members(cardinality);
        if (runtime.rank == owner)
            devices[(seed / runtime.ranks) % device_count]->getMembers(seed, members);
        checkMpi(MPI_Bcast(members.data(), cardinality, MPI_INT, owner, MPI_COMM_WORLD));
        forDevices(device_count, [&](int i) { devices[i]->remove(members); });
        remaining -= cardinality;
        if (runtime.rank == 0) clusters.push_back({std::move(members), seed});
    }
    return clusters;
}

// QT Clustering Benchmark - MPI / OpenMP / CUDA
// 
// QT (Quality Threshold) clustering is an algorithm that builds clusters
// by starting with a seed point and iteratively adding the closest point
// that maintains the cluster's diameter below a threshold.

#include <algorithm>
#include <chrono>
#include <cfloat>
#include <climits>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <limits>
#include <memory>
#include <stdexcept>
#include <string>
#include <vector>

#include <cuda_runtime.h>
#include <mpi.h>
#include <omp.h>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

static const double MAX_WIDTH = 20.0;
static const double MAX_HEIGHT = 20.0;

// Structure to represent a point in 2D space
struct Point {
    double x, y;
};

// Structure to represent a cluster
struct Cluster {
    std::vector<int> members;
    int seed_point;
};

// Generate synthetic 2D point data in clusters
void generateSyntheticData(std::vector<Point>& points, const int N, unsigned int seed = 42) {
    auto frand = [&seed]() mutable { return rand_r(&seed) / static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX); };
    
    const double min_dim = std::min(MAX_WIDTH, MAX_HEIGHT);
    int count = 0;
    
    while (count < N) {
        // Create group_cnt points within a circle of radius R
        // around center point (cntr_x, cntr_y)
        const double cntr_x = frand() * MAX_WIDTH;
        const double cntr_y = frand() * MAX_HEIGHT;
        const double R = frand() * min_dim / 2.0;
        int group_cnt = static_cast<int>(frand() * (N / 30.0));
        
        // For N <= 30 the original expression always produces zero.
        if (N <= 30) group_cnt = 1;

        // Make sure we don't make more points than we need
        if (group_cnt > (N - count)) {
            group_cnt = N - count;
        }
        
        while (group_cnt > 0) {
            const double sign = (frand() < 0.5) ? -1.0 : 1.0;
            const double r = frand() * R;
            const double dx = (2.0 * frand() - 1.0) * r;
            const double dy = std::sqrt(r * r - dx * dx) * sign;
            const double x = cntr_x + dx;
            const double y = cntr_y + dy;
            
            if (x < 0 || x > MAX_WIDTH || y < 0 || y > MAX_HEIGHT) {
                continue;
            }
            
            points[count] = {x, y};
            count++;
            group_cnt--;
        }
    }
}

// Keep the same double-precision distances, strict threshold, and lowest-index
// tie breaking as the sequential algorithm. In particular, comparing squared
// distances instead would change ties caused by square-root rounding.
__host__ __device__ inline double distance(const Point& p1, const Point& p2) {
    const double dx = p1.x - p2.x;
    const double dy = p1.y - p2.y;
#ifdef __CUDA_ARCH__
    return __dsqrt_rn(dx * dx + dy * dy);
#else
    return std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);
#endif
}

static void checkCuda(cudaError_t status) {
    if (status != cudaSuccess) throw std::runtime_error(cudaGetErrorString(status));
}

static void checkMpi(int status) {
    if (status != MPI_SUCCESS) {
        char message[MPI_MAX_ERROR_STRING];
        int length = 0;
        MPI_Error_string(status, message, &length);
        fprintf(stderr, "MPI: %.*s\n", length, message);
        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, status);
    }
}

// MPI calls stay on the initial thread; OpenMP workers manage independent CUDA
// devices. A rank can use several GPUs, and local ranks divide visible devices.
struct ParallelRuntime {
    int rank = 0, ranks = 1, local_rank = 0, local_ranks = 1;
    std::vector<int> devices;
    int device_sharers = 1;

    ParallelRuntime(int& argc, char**& argv) {
        int provided = MPI_THREAD_SINGLE;
        checkMpi(MPI_Init_thread(&argc, &argv, MPI_THREAD_FUNNELED, &provided));
        checkMpi(MPI_Comm_set_errhandler(MPI_COMM_WORLD, MPI_ERRORS_RETURN));
        checkMpi(MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank));
        checkMpi(MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &ranks));
        if (provided < MPI_THREAD_FUNNELED) {
            if (rank == 0) fprintf(stderr, "MPI_THREAD_FUNNELED is required.\n");
            MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);
        }
    }

    void initializeDevices() {
        MPI_Comm local_comm;
            const int member = clusters[c].members[i];
            if (membership[member] >= 0) {
                printf("ERROR: Point %d appears in multiple clusters (%d and %zu)\n",
                       member, membership[member], c);
                valid = false;
            }
            membership[member] = static_cast<int>(c);
        }
    }
    
    // Count clustered points
    int clustered_count = 0;
    for (size_t i = 0; i < membership.size(); ++i) {
        if (membership[i] >= 0) clustered_count++;
    }
    
    printf("Total points: %zu, Clustered: %d, Unclustered: %zu\n",
           points.size(), clustered_count, points.size() - clustered_count);
    
    return valid;
}

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -n <num>     Number of points (default: 1000)\n");
    printf("  -t <float>   Distance threshold for clustering (default: 2.0)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    ParallelRuntime runtime(argc, argv);
    try {
    int num_points = 1000;
    double threshold = 2.0;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;
    
    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            num_points = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-t") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            threshold = atof(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            if (runtime.rank == 0) printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            if (runtime.rank == 0) {
                printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
                printUsage(argv[0]);
            }
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    if (num_points <= 0 || threshold <= 0.0) {
        if (runtime.rank == 0) printf("Error: Invalid parameters (num_points=%d, threshold=%.2f)\n", 
               num_points, threshold);
        return 1;
    }
    
    runtime.initializeDevices();
    if (runtime.rank == 0) {
    printf("QT Clustering Benchmark\n");
    printf("Number of points: %d\n", num_points);
    printf("Distance threshold: %.2f\n", threshold);
    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
    }
    
    // Generate synthetic data
    std::vector<Point> points(num_points);
    if (runtime.rank == 0) generateSyntheticData(points, num_points);
    // MPI counts are ints; chunking also supports datasets beyond INT_MAX bytes.
    unsigned char* point_bytes = reinterpret_cast<unsigned char*>(points.data());
    const size_t point_bytes_count = points.size() * sizeof(Point);
    for (size_t offset = 0; offset < point_bytes_count;) {
        const int count = static_cast<int>(std::min<size_t>(point_bytes_count - offset, INT_MAX));
        checkMpi(MPI_Bcast(point_bytes + offset, count, MPI_BYTE, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD));
        offset += count;
    }
    checkMpi(MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD));
    
    // Perform QT clustering
    auto cluster_start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    
    const std::vector<Cluster> clusters = qtClustering(points, threshold, runtime);
    
    auto cluster_end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    
    const double local_seconds = std::chrono::duration<double>(cluster_end - cluster_start).count();
    double elapsed_seconds = 0.0;
    checkMpi(MPI_Reduce(&local_seconds, &elapsed_seconds, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MAX, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD));
    if (runtime.rank != 0) return 0;
    printf("Clustering time: %lld ms\n", static_cast<long long>(elapsed_seconds * 1000.0));
    printf("Clusters found: %zu\n", clusters.size());
    
    // Calculate statistics and performance metrics
    int total_clustered = 0;
    int max_cluster_size = 0;
    
    for (size_t i = 0; i < clusters.size(); ++i) {
        const int size = static_cast<int>(clusters[i].members.size());
        total_clustered += size;
        max_cluster_size = std::max(max_cluster_size, size);
    }
    
    const double avg_cluster_size = clusters.empty() ? 0.0 : 
        static_cast<double>(total_clustered) / clusters.size();
    
    printf("Points clustered: %d / %d (%.1f%%)\n", 
           total_clustered, num_points, 
           100.0 * total_clustered / num_points);
    printf("Average cluster size: %.2f\n", avg_cluster_size);
    printf("Maximum cluster size: %d\n", max_cluster_size);
    
    // Performance metrics
    const double time_sec = elapsed_seconds;
    const double clusters_per_sec = clusters.size() / time_sec;
    const double points_per_sec = num_points / time_sec;
    printf("Performance: %.1f clusters/s, %.1f points/s\n", 
           clusters_per_sec, points_per_sec);
    
    // Print results for external validation
    if (printResults) {
        // Serialize cluster membership for hashing
        std::vector<double> membershipData;
        membershipData.reserve(num_points);
        std::vector<int> membership(num_points, -1);
        for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {
            for (size_t i = 0; i < clusters[c].members.size(); ++i) {
                membership[clusters[c].members[i]] = static_cast<int>(c);
            }
        }
        for (int m : membership) {
            membershipData.push_back(static_cast<double>(m));
        }
        print_results(membershipData, "ClusterMembership");
    }
    
    // Validation
    if (validate) {
        const bool valid = validateClusters(clusters, points, threshold);
        
        if (valid) {
            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    return 0;
    } catch (const std::exception& e) {
        fprintf(stderr, "Rank %d: %s\n", runtime.rank, e.what());
        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);
        return 1;
    }
}

    $<$<COMPILE_LANGUAGE:CUDA>:-O3;--fmad=false;-Xcompiler=-O3,-march=native,-ffp-contract=off,-Wall,-Wextra>

Path('/tmp/qtclustering-check.cu').write_text(\"\\n#include <cuda_runtime.h>\\nstatic size_t test_memory_limit = 0;\\nstatic cudaError_t testMemoryInfo(size_t* free_bytes, size_t* total_bytes) {\\n    cudaError_t result = cudaMemGetInfo(free_bytes, total_bytes);\\n    if (result == cudaSuccess && test_memory_limit) *free_bytes = test_memory_limit;\\n    return result;\\n}\\n#define cudaMemGetInfo testMemoryInfo\\n#define main benchmarkMain\\n#include \\\"/home/llmtest/evals/20261006-204623/qtclustering_gpt-6.1-sol-xhigh_hybrid_r5/qtclustering/qtclustering.cpp\\\"\\n#undef main\\n#undef cudaMemGetInfo\\n// Calculate Euclidean referenceDistance between two points\\ninline double referenceDistance(const Point& p1, const Point& p2) {\\n    double dx = p1.x - p2.x;\\n    double dy = p1.y - p2.y;\\n    return std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);\\n}\\n\\n// Find the closest unclustered point to the current cluster that maintains diameter < threshold\\n// Returns -1 if no such point exists\\nint findClosestPoint(const std::vector<int>& cluster_members,\\n                     const std::vector<bool>& clustered,\\n                     const std::vector<bool>& in_cluster,\\n                     const std::vector<Point>& points,\\n                     const double threshold,\\n                     const int point_count) {\\n    int closest_point = -1;\\n    double min_diameter = std::numeric_limits<double>::max();\\n    \\n    // Try each unclustered point as a candidate\\n    for (int candidate = 0; candidate < point_count; ++candidate) {\\n        // Skip if already clustered or already in this cluster\\n        if (clustered[candidate] || in_cluster[candidate]) continue;\\n        \\n        // Calculate the maximum referenceDistance from candidate to all cluster members\\n        double max_dist = 0.0;\\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < cluster_members.size(); ++i) {\\n            const int member = cluster_members[i];\\n            const double dist = referenceDistance(points[candidate], points[member]);\\n            max_dist = std::max(max_dist, dist);\\n        }\\n        \\n        // If adding this point keeps diameter below threshold and is better than current best\\n        if (max_dist < threshold && max_dist < min_diameter) {\\n            min_diameter = max_dist;\\n            closest_point = candidate;\\n        }\\n    }\\n    \\n    return closest_point;\\n}\\n\\n// Generate a candidate cluster starting from a seed point\\n// Returns the cardinality (size) of the cluster\\nint generateCandidateCluster(const int seed_point,\\n                              const std::vector<bool>& clustered,\\n                              const std::vector<Point>& points,\\n                              const double threshold,\\n                              const int point_count,\\n                              std::vector<int>* cluster_members = nullptr) {\\n    std::vector<bool> in_cluster(point_count, false);\\n    std::vector<int> members;\\n    \\n    // Add seed point\\n    in_cluster[seed_point] = true;\\n    members.push_back(seed_point);\\n    \\n    // Iteratively add closest points\\n    while (static_cast<int>(members.size()) < point_count) {\\n        // Find closest point to current cluster that maintains diameter < threshold\\n        const int closest = findClosestPoint(members, clustered, in_cluster, points, \\n                                             threshold, point_count);\\n        \\n        if (closest < 0) break; // No more points can be added\\n        \\n        in_cluster[closest] = true;\\n        members.push_back(closest);\\n    }\\n    \\n    // Copy members if requested\\n    if (cluster_members) {\\n        *cluster_members = members;\\n    }\\n    \\n    return static_cast<int>(members.size());\\n}\\n\\n// Main QT clustering algorithm\\nstd::vector<Cluster> referenceClustering(const std::vector<Point>& points,\\n                                  const double threshold) {\\n    const int N = static_cast<int>(points.size());\\n    std::vector<bool> clustered(N, false);\\n    std::vector<int> unclustered_indices;\\n    std::vector<Cluster> clusters;\\n    \\n    // Initialize unclustered indices\\n    for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) {\\n        unclustered_indices.push_back(i);\\n    }\\n    \\n    // Main clustering loop\\n    while ("'!unclustered_indices.empty()) {'"\\n        int max_cardinality = -1;\\n        int best_seed = -1;\\n        std::vector<int> best_cluster_members;\\n        \\n        // Try each unclustered point as a seed\\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < unclustered_indices.size(); ++i) {\\n            const int seed = unclustered_indices[i];\\n            if (clustered[seed]) continue;\\n            \\n            std::vector<int> candidate_members;\\n            const int cardinality = generateCandidateCluster(seed, clustered, points, \\n                                                       threshold, N, \\n                                                       &candidate_members);\\n            \\n            if (cardinality > max_cardinality) {\\n                max_cardinality = cardinality;\\n                best_seed = seed;\\n                best_cluster_members = candidate_members;\\n            }\\n        }\\n        \\n        // If we found a cluster, add it\\n        if (best_seed >= 0 && max_cardinality > 0) {\\n            Cluster cluster;\\n            cluster.seed_point = best_seed;\\n            cluster.members = best_cluster_members;\\n            clusters.push_back(cluster);\\n            \\n            // Mark all members as clustered\\n            for (size_t i = 0; i < best_cluster_members.size(); ++i) {\\n                clustered[best_cluster_members[i]] = true;\\n            }\\n            \\n            // Remove clustered points from unclustered list\\n            unclustered_indices.erase(\\n                std::remove_if(unclustered_indices.begin(), unclustered_indices.end(),\\n                              [&clustered](int idx) { return clustered[idx]; }),\\n                unclustered_indices.end()\\n            );\\n        } else {\\n            // No more clusters can be formed\\n            break;\\n        }\\n    }\\n    \\n    return clusters;\\n}\\n\\n\\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\\n    ParallelRuntime runtime(argc, argv);\\n    try {\\n        runtime.initializeDevices();\\n        int failures = 0, tested = 0;\\n        auto check = [&](std::vector<Point> points, double threshold, size_t memory = 0, bool obvious = false) {\\n            test_memory_limit = memory;\\n            const auto actual = qtClustering(points, threshold, runtime);\\n            int bad = 0;\\n            if (runtime.rank == 0) {\\n                std::vector<Cluster> expected;\\n                if (obvious) {\\n                    expected.push_back({{}, 0});\\n                    for (int i = 0; i < static_cast<int>(points.size()); ++i) expected[0].members.push_back(i);\\n                } else expected = referenceClustering(points, threshold);\\n                bad = actual.size() "'!= expected.size();'"\\n                for (size_t c = 0; "'!bad && c < actual.size(); ++c)'"\\n                    bad = actual[c].seed_point "'!= expected[c].seed_point || actual[c].members != expected[c].members;'"\\n                printf(\\\"Case %d: n=%zu threshold=%.17g memory=%zu %s\\\\n\\\", tested, points.size(), threshold, memory, bad ? \\\"FAIL\\\" : \\\"PASS\\\");\\n            }\\n            checkMpi(MPI_Bcast(&bad, 1, MPI_INT, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD));\\n            failures += bad;\\n            ++tested;\\n        };\\n        check({{0, 0}}, 1);\\n        check(std::vector<Point>(9, {1, 1}), 1);\\n        check({{0, 0}, {1, 0}, {2, 0}, {3, 0}}, 1);\\n        check({{0, 0}, {1, 0}, {2, 0}, {3, 0}}, std::nextafter(1.0, 2.0));\\n        check({{0, 0}, {1, std::ldexp(1.0, -26)}, {1, 0}, {-1, 0}, {0, 1}}, 2);\\n        check({{0, 0}, {1, std::ldexp(1.0, -26)}, {1, 0}, {-1, 0}, {0, 1}}, std::nextafter(1.0, 2.0));\\n        check({{0, 0}, {0, 0}, {1, 0}}, std::numeric_limits<double>::min());\\n        check({{0, 0}, {0, 0}, {1, 0}}, std::numeric_limits<double>::quiet_NaN());\\n        for (int n : {31, 65, 127, 256}) {\\n            std::vector<Point> points(n);\\n            generateSyntheticData(points, n);\\n            for (double threshold : {0.1, 2.0, 5.0}) check(points, threshold);\\n            check(points, 5.0, 8192);\\n        }\\n        check(std::vector<Point>(1030, {2, 3}), 1, 0, true);\\n        check(std::vector<Point>(1030, {2, 3}), 1, 24576, true);\\n        if (runtime.rank == 0) printf(\\\"Checked %d cases;
